Entry530/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 626
English
Isnad

Al-Qasim b. 'Abdullah reported to me that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said '

Matn

The signs of a hypocrite are three: when he speaks, he lies; when he promises, he breaks it; and when he is entrusted, he betrays.' This is in the Book of God: 'When the hypocrites come to you, they say: We testify that you are indeed the Messenger of God. God knows that you are indeed His Messenger, and God testifies that the hypocrites are indeed liars.' (Al-Munafiqun: 1) And He said: 'Among them are those who made a covenant with God: If He gives us from His bounty, we will surely give charity and be among the righteous. But when He gave them from His bounty, they became stingy with it and turned away, averse. So He penalized them with hypocrisy in their hearts until the Day they meet Him, because they failed God in what they promised Him and because they were lying.' (At-Tawbah: 75-77)

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي الْقَاسِمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: "

Matn

آيَةُ الْمُنَافِقِ ثَلَاثٌ: إِنْ حَدَّثَ كَذَبَ، وَإِنْ وَعَدَ أَخْلَفَ، وَإِنِ اؤْتُمِنَ خَانَ "، وَذَلِكَ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ قَالَ: {إِذَا جَاءَكَ الْمُنَافِقُونَ قَالُوا نَشْهَدُ إِنَّكَ لَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ وَاللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ إِنَّكَ لَرَسُولُهُ وَاللَّهُ يَشْهَدُ إِنَّ الْمُنَافِقِينَ لَكَاذِبُونَ} [المنافقون: 1] ، وَقَالَ: {وَمِنْهُمْ مَنْ عَاهَدَ اللَّهَ لَئِنْ آتَانَا مِنْ فَضْلِهِ لَنَصَّدَّقَنَّ وَلَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ فَلَمَّا آتَاهُمْ مِنْ فَضْلِهِ بَخِلُوا بِهِ وَتَوَلَّوْا وَهُمْ مُعْرِضُونَ فَأَعْقَبَهُمْ نِفَاقًا فِي قُلُوبِهِمْ إِلَى يَوْمِ يَلْقَوْنَهُ بِمَا أَخْلَفُوا اللَّهَ مَا وَعَدُوهُ وَبِمَا كَانُوا يَكْذِبُونَ} [التوبة: 76] "

  2. 02Al-Qasim b. Abdullahالْقَاسِمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ
  3. 03Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry531/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 626
English
Isnad

Usamah b. Zayd reported to us from Salih b. Kaysan, from Sa'd b. Ibrahim, from Abu Salamah b. 'Abd Al-Rahman, that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said '

Matn

A reformer is not a liar.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنَا أُسَامَةُ بْنُ زَيْدٍ، عَنْ صَالِحِ بْنِ كَيْسَانَ، عَنْ سَعْدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: «

Matn

لَيْسَ الْمُصْلِحُ بِالْكَذَّابِ»

Entry532/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 628
English
Isnad

No isnad: the text is given as a direct statement.

Matn

O people, what makes you persist in following lies as moths swarm into a fire? Every lie is recorded against the son of Adam except in three cases: a man’s lie to his wife to please her, a person’s lie between two people to reconcile them, and a lie in the stratagems of war.

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي دَاوُدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنِ ابْنِ خُثَيْمٍ، عَنْ شَهْرِ بْنِ حَوْشَبٍ، عَنْ أَسْمَاءَ ابْنَةِ يَزِيدَ الْأَنْصَارِيَّةِ، قَالَتْ: سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، يَخْطُبُ لِلنَّاسِ، فَقَالَ: "

Matn

يَا أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ مَا يَحْمِلُكُمْ عَلَى أَنْ تَتَابَعُوا فِي الْكَذِبِ، كَمَا يَتَتَابَعُ الْفَرَاشُ فِي النَّارِ، كُلُّ الْكَذِبِ يُكْتَبُ عَلَى ابْنِ آدَمَ إِلَّا ثَلَاثًا: كَذِبُ الرَّجُلِ عَلَى امْرَأَتِهِ لِيُرْضِيَهَا، وَرَجُلٌ كَذَبَ بَيْنَ اثْنَيْنِ لِيُصْلِحَ بَيْنَهُمَا، وَرَجُلٌ كَذَبَ فِي خَدِيعَةِ حَرْبٍ "

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterسَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Asma bint Yazid al-Ansariyyahعَنْ أَسْمَاءَ ابْنَةِ يَزِيدَ الْأَنْصَارِيَّةِ
  3. 03Shahr b. Hawshabعَنْ شَهْرِ بْنِ حَوْشَبٍ
  4. 04Ibn Khuthaymعَنِ ابْنِ خُثَيْمٍ
  5. 05Dawud b. Abd al-Rahmanدَاوُدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry533/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 630
English
Isnad

Muhammad b. Muslim reported to me from Ayyub Al-Sakhtiyani, from Ibn Sirin, from 'Aishah, the wife of the Prophet, ﷺ, who said '

Matn

Nothing was more detested by the companions of the Messenger of God, ﷺ, than lying... from anyone, even if it was little, until it led him to repent.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ مُسْلِمٍ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ السَّخْتِيَانِيِّ، عَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام ، قَالَتْ: «

Matn

مَا كَانَ شَيْءٌ أَبْغَضَ عِنْدَ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ، صلى الله عليه وسلم ، مِنَ الْكَذِبِ (. . . . . . . . .) مِنْ أَحَدٍ مِنْ شَيْءٍ، وَإِنْ قَلَّ فَيُخْرِجُ لَهُ مِنْ نَفْسِهِ حَتَّى تُحْدِثَ لَهُ تَوْبَةً»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Aishah, wife of the ProphetEarliest transmitterعَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ
  2. 02Ibn Sirinعَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ
  3. 03Ayyub al-Sakhtiyaniعَنْ أَيُّوبَ السَّخْتِيَانِيِّ
  4. 04Muhammad b. Muslimمُحَمَّدُ بْنُ مُسْلِمٍ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry534/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 631
English
Isnad

Malik reported to me from Safwan b. Sulaym, from 'Ata' b. Yasar,

Matn

that a man said to the Messenger of God, ﷺ: 'O Messenger of God, should I lie to my wife?' He said: 'There is no good in lying.' The man said: 'O Messenger of God, should I promise her and tell her?' The Messenger of God, ﷺ, said: 'There is no sin on you.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ سُلَيْمٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ عليه السلام: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَكْذِبُ امْرَأَتِي؟ قَالَ: «لَا خَيْرَ فِي الْكَذِبِ» ، فَقَالَ الرَّجُلُ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَعِدُهَا وَأَقُولُ لَهَا؟ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ، صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكَ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterلِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Ata b. Yasarعَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ
  3. 03Safwan b. Sulaymعَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ سُلَيْمٍ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي

Entry536/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 632
English
Isnad

Sulayman b. Bilal reported to me from Kathir b. Zayd, from the woman who

Matn

bore Muhriz b. Zuhayr a child—Muhriz was a man of Aslam and a Companion of the Prophet—who said that she used to hear Muhriz say: “O God, I seek refuge in You from the age of liars.” She asked him, “What is the age of liars?” He replied, “An age in which lying becomes widespread, so that a person who does not intend to lie begins speaking as they do, and thereby becomes involved in their lies.”

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ بِلَالٍ، عَنْ كَثِيرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ، عَنْ أُمِّ وَلَدِ مُحْرِزِ بْنِ زُهَيْرٍ، رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَسْلَمَ مِنْ أَصْحَابِ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام، أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ تَسْمَعُ مُحْرِزًا يَقُولُ: «

Matn

اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنْ زَمَانِ الْكَاذِبِينَ» ، قَالَتْ: فَقُلْتُ لَهُ: وَمَا زَمَانُ الْكَاذِبِينَ؟ قَالَ: «زَمَانٌ يَظْهَرُ فِيهِ الْكَذِبُ فَيَذْهَبُ الَّذِي لَا يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَكْذِبَ، فَيَتَحَدَّثُ بِحَدِيثِهِمْ، فَإِذَا هُوَ قَدْ دَخَلَ مَعَهُمْ فِي كَذِبِهِمْ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Muhriz b. Zuhayr (a man from Aslam and companion of the Prophet)Earliest transmitterمُحْرِزًا
  2. 02The unnamed freed slave woman of Muhriz b. Zuhayrعَنْ أُمِّ وَلَدِ مُحْرِزِ بْنِ زُهَيْرٍ
  3. 03Kathir b. Zaydعَنْ كَثِيرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ
  4. 04Sulayman b. Bilalسُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ بِلَالٍ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry537/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 633
English
Isnad

Ibn Lahi‘ah reported to me from Abi al-Aswad, from ‘Abd Allah b. Rafi‘, from Abi Hurayrah, that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said '

Matn

Faith and disbelief do not gather in the heart of a person, nor do truth and falsehood combine, nor do treachery and trustworthiness come together.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي الْأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: «

Matn

لَا يَجْتَمِعُ الْإِيمَانُ وَالْكُفْرُ فِي قَلْبِ امْرِئٍ، وَلَا يَجْتَمِعُ الصِّدْقُ وَالْكَذِبُ جَمِيعًا، وَلَا تَجْتَمِعُ الْخِيَانَةُ وَالْأَمَانَةُ جَمِيعًا»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Abu Hurayrahعَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ
  3. 03Abdullah b. Rafiعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ
  4. 04Abu al-Aswadعَنْ أَبِي الْأَسْوَدِ
  5. 05Ibn Lahiahابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry538/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 633
English
Isnad

Ibn Lahi'ah reported to me from Abu Qabil, who said:

Matn

I heard a man say that he heard 'Abd Allah b. 'Amr b. al-'As say: ‘In Hell there is a prison, and in Paradise there is a fortress of pearl. No one who has any falsehood in him will enter the fortress of Paradise. The prison of Hell is entered only by the worst of the wicked: its floor is fire, its roof is fire, its walls are fire, and flames scorch within it.’

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي قَبِيلٍ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ رَجُلًا، يَقُولُ: إنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ، يَقُولُ: «

Matn

إِنَّ فِي النَّارِ سِجْنًا، وَإِنَّ فِي الْجَنَّةِ حِصْنًا مِنْ لُؤْلُؤٍ، فَأَمَّا حِصْنُ الْجَنَّةِ فَلَا يَدْخُلُهُ مَنْ كَانَ فِيهِ مِنَ الْكَذِبِ شَيْءٌ، وَأَمَّا سِجْنُ النَّارِ فَلَا يَدْخُلُهُ إِلَّا شَرُّ الْأَشْرَارِ، قَرَارُهُ نَارٌ، وَسَقْفُهُ نَارُ، وَجُدْرَانُهُ نَارٌ، وَتَلْفَحُ فِيهِ نَارٌ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abdullah b. Amr b. al-AsEarliest transmitterسَمِعَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ
  2. 02An unnamed manسَمِعْتُ رَجُلًا
  3. 03Abu Qabilعَنْ أَبِي قَبِيلٍ
  4. 04Ibn Lahiahابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Isnad

Jarir b. Hazim reported to me from Bahz b. Hakim, from his father, from his grandfather, who said: I heard the Messenger of God, ﷺ, say: '

Matn

Isnad

Matn

وَيْلٌ لِلَّذِي يُحَدِّثُ الْقَوْمَ كَاذِبًا لِيُضْحِكَهُمْ، وَيْلٌ لَهُ، وَيْلٌ لَهُ» وَيْلٌ لَهُ "

Entry540/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 635
English
Isnad

Hafs b. Maysarah reported to me from Zayd b. Aslam that a man said to the Messenger of God, ﷺ: '

Matn

I tell stories to make people laugh, some of which are false.' The Messenger of God, ﷺ, said: 'There is no good in falsehood.' The man said: 'O Messenger of God, you jest and laugh.' He replied: 'I only speak the truth.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي حَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَجُلًا، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ عليه السلام: "

Matn

إِنِّي أُحَدِّثُ الْحَدِيثَ أُضْحِكُ بِهِ الْقَوْمَ بَعْضُهُ بَاطِلٌ، قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام: «لَا خَيْرَ فِي الْبَاطِلِ» . فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنَّكَ مُمَازِحٌ وَضَاحِكٌ، فَقَالَ: «إِنِّي لَا أَقُولُ إِلَّا حَقًّا»

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterلِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Zayd b. Aslamعَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ
  3. 03Hafs b. Maysarahحَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ

Isnad

'Amr b. al-Harith reported to me that Sahl b. 'Aqil told him:

Matn

The Prophet Isma'il had promised a man to meet him at a certain place. The man came but forgot, so Isma'il stayed there, spending the night, until the man returned the next day. He asked, ‘Have you been here all this time?’ The man replied, ‘Yes.’ Isma'il said, ‘I would not leave until you came.’ That is why he was true to his promise.

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي عَمْرُو بْنُ الْحَارِثِ، أَنَّ سَهْلَ بْنَ عَقِيلٍ حَدَّثَهُ، "

Matn

أَنَّ إِسْمَاعِيلَ النَّبِيَّ وَعَدَ رَجُلًا مَكَانًا أَنْ يَأْتِيَهُ فَجَاءَ وَنَسِيَ الرَّجُلُ، فَظَلَّ بِهِ إِسْمَاعِيلُ وَبَاتَ حَتَّى جَاءَ الرَّجُلُ مِنَ الْغَدِ، فَقَالَ: مَا بَرِحْتَ مِنْ هَاهُنَا؟ قَالَ: لَا، قَالَ: «لَمْ أَكُنْ لِأَبْرَحَ حَتَّى تَأْتِيَ فَبِذَلِكَ كَانَ صَادِقَ الْوَعْدِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Sahl b. AqilEarliest transmitterأَنَّ سَهْلَ بْنَ عَقِيلٍ
  2. 02Amr b. al-Harithعَمْرُو بْنُ الْحَارِثِ
  3. 03Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 639
English
Isnad

Jarir b. Hazim reported to me from Mujalid b. Sa‘id, from Qays b. Abi Hazim, who said: I heard Abu Bakr al-Siddiq addressing the people, saying: '

Matn

O people, beware of lying, for lying is incompatible with faith.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي جَرِيرُ بْنُ حَازِمٍ، عَنْ مُجَالِدِ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ أَبِي حَازِمٍ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ أَبَا بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقَ، وَهُوَ يَخْطُبُ النَّاسَ وَهُوَ يَقُولُ: «

Matn

أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ إِيَّاكُمْ وَالْكَذِبَ، فَإِنَّ الْكَذِبَ مُجَانِبُ الْإِيمَانِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu Bakr al-SiddiqEarliest transmitterسَمِعْتُ أَبَا بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقَ
  2. 02Qays b. Abi Hazimعَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ أَبِي حَازِمٍ
  3. 03Mujalid b. Saidعَنْ مُجَالِدِ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ
  4. 04Jarir b. Hazimجَرِيرُ بْنُ حَازِمٍ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي

Entry545/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 640
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Nashit reported to me from Qays b. Rafi‘, from ‘Abd al-Rahman b. Jubayr, from ‘Abd Allah b. ‘Amr b. al-‘As, who said '

Matn

A servant does not attain complete faith until he eats only from lawful earnings, completes his ablution in difficult circumstances, and abandons lying even in jest.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ نَشِيطٍ، عَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ، قَالَ: «

Matn

لَا يُؤْمِنُ الْعَبْدُ كُلَّ الْإِيمَانِ حَتَّى لَا يَأْكُلَ إِلَّا كَسْبًا، وَيُتِمَّ الْوُضُوءَ فِي الْمَكَارِهِ، وَيَضَعَ الْكَذِبَ وَلَوْ فِي الْمُزَاحَةِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abdullah b. Amr b. al-AsEarliest transmitterعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ
  2. 02Abd al-Rahman b. Jubayrعَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ
  3. 03Qays b. Rafiعَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ
  4. 04Ibrahim b. Nashitإِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ نَشِيطٍ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry546/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 641
English
Isnad

Ibn Lahi‘ah reported to me from al-Harith b. Yazid, from ‘Abd Allah b. ‘Amr b. al-‘As, that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said '

Matn

There are four traits that, if you possess them, you need not care about what you miss in this world: safeguarding a trust, truthful speech, good character, and purity in sustenance.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ، عَنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: "

Matn

أَرْبَعٌ إِذَا كُنَّ فِيكَ فَلَا عَلَيْكَ مَا فَاتَكَ مِنَ الدُّنْيَا: حَفِظُ أَمَانَةٍ، وَصِدْقُ حَدِيثٍ، وَحُسْنُ خَلِيقَةٍ، وَعِفَّةُ طُعْمَةٍ "

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Abdullah b. Amr b. al-Asعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ
  3. 03Al-Harith b. Yazidعَنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ
  4. 04Ibn Lahiahابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry547/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 642
English
Isnad

Musa b. ‘Ulayy reported to me from his father, from ‘Abd Allah b. ‘Amr b. al-‘As, who said '

Matn

There are four traits that, if you are granted them, it will not harm you what you are deprived of in this world.' Then he mentioned these same traits.

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي مُوسَى بْنُ عُلَيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ، قَالَ: «

Matn

أَرْبَعٌ إِذَا أُعْطِيتَهُنَّ فَلَا يَضُرُّكَ مَا عُزِلَ عَنْكَ مِنَ الدُّنْيَا، ثُمَّ ذَكَرَ هَذِهِ الْخِصَالَ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abdullah b. Amr b. al-AsEarliest transmitterعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ
  2. 02His fatherعَنْ أَبِيهِ
  3. 03Musa b. Ulayyمُوسَى بْنُ عُلَيِّ
  4. 04Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي

Entry548/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 643
English
Isnad

Ibn Lahi‘ah reported to me from Ibn Hubayrah, from Abi ‘Abd al-Rahman, from ‘Abd Allah b. ‘Amr b. al-‘As, who said '

Matn

There are three traits that, if found in someone other than you, you should not hesitate to testify that he is a hypocrite: if he lies when he speaks, breaks his promise, and betrays when trusted. But if he speaks the truth, fulfills his trust, and keeps his promise, do not hesitate to testify that he is a believer.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ هُبَيْرَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ، قَالَ: "

Matn

ثَلَاثٌ إِذَا كُنَّ فِي غَيْرِكَ فَلَا تَتَحَرَّجَنَّ أَنْ تَشْهَدَ عَلَيْهِ أَنَّهُ مُنَافِقٌ: مَنْ كَانَ إِذَا حَدَّثَ كَذَبَ، وَإِذَا وَعَدَ أَخْلَفَ، وَإِذَا اؤْتُمِنَ خَانَ. وَمَنْ كَانَ إِذَا حَدَّثَ صَدَقَ، وَإِذَا اؤْتُمِنَ أَدَّى، وَإِذَا وَعَدَ أَوْفَى فَلَا تَتَحَرَّجْ أَنْ تَشْهَدَ أَنَّهُ مُؤْمِنٌ "

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abdullah b. Amr b. al-AsEarliest transmitterعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْعَاصِ
  2. 02Abu Abd al-Rahmanعَنْ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  3. 03Ibn Hubayrahعَنِ ابْنِ هُبَيْرَةَ
  4. 04Ibn Lahiahابْنُ لَهِيعَةَ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 644
English
Isnad

Yahya b. Ayyub reported to me from Ibn ‘Ajlan that the Messenger of God, ﷺ,

Matn

would recognize a lie from some of his companions and would remain distant and displeased with him until he knew that he had repented from it.

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَجْلَانَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام «

Matn

كَانَ يَعْرِفُ الْكِذْبَةَ مِنْ بَعْضِ أَصْحَابِهِ، فَمَا يَزَالُ عَنْهُ مُعْرِضًا سَاخِطًا عَلَيْهِ حَتَّى يَعْرِفَ أَنَّهُ قَدْ تَابَ مِنْهَا»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Ibn Ajlanعَنِ ابْنِ عَجْلَانَ
  3. 03Yahya b. Ayyubيَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ
  4. 04Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry550/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 645
English
Isnad

Shabib b. Sa‘id reported to me from Shu‘bah b. al-Hajjaj, from Mansur and Sulayman, from Abi Wa’il, from ‘Abd Allah b. Mas‘ud, that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said '

Matn

A servant continues to speak the truth and strive for truth until he is recorded as truthful. And a servant continues to lie and strive in lying until he is recorded as a liar.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي شَبِيبُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ بْنِ الْحَجَّاجِ، عَنْ مَنْصُورٍ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي وَائِلٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: «

Matn

لَا يَزَالُ الْعَبْدُ يَصْدُقُ وَيَتَحَرَّى الصِّدْقَ حَتَّى يُكتَبَ صَدِيقًا، وَلَا يَزَالُ الْعَبْدُ يَكْذِبُ وَيَتَحَرَّى الْكَذِبَ حَتَّى يُكْتَبَ كَاذِبًا»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Abdullah b. Masudعَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ
  3. 03Abu Wailعَنْ أَبِي وَائِلٍ
  4. 04Sulayman (parallel transmitter)وَسُلَيْمَانَ
  5. 05Mansur (parallel transmitter)عَنْ مَنْصُورٍ
  6. 06Shubah b. al-Hajjajعَنْ شُعْبَةَ بْنِ الْحَجَّاجِ
  7. 07Shabib b. Saidشَبِيبُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ
  8. 08Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

English
Isnad

Shabib reported to me from Shu‘bah, from someone who told him, from Samurah b. Jundub, that the Prophet, ﷺ, said '

Matn

Whoever narrates a hadith from me thinking it is a lie is one of the two liars.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي شَبِيبٌ، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ مَنْ، حَدَّثَهُ، عَنْ سَمُرَةَ بْنِ جُنْدُبٍ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ ، عليه السلام ، قَالَ: «

Matn

مَنْ رَوَى عَنِّي حَدِيثًا يَرَى أَنَّهُ كَذِبٌ فَهُوَ أَحَدُ الْكَاذِبَيْنِ»

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The ProphetEarliest transmitterأَنَّ النَّبِيَّ
  2. 02Samurah b. Jundubعَنْ سَمُرَةَ بْنِ جُنْدُبٍ
  3. 03An unnamed transmitter who informed Shubahعَنْ مَنْ، حَدَّثَهُ
  4. 04Shubahعَنْ شُعْبَةَ
  5. 05Shabibشَبِيبٌ

Entry552/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 647
English
Isnad

Shabib reported to me from Rawh b. al-Qasim, from ‘Asim b. Bahdalah, from al-Musayyib b. Rafi‘, from Ibn Mas‘ud, who said '

Matn

Satan meets a man in the form of a human and tells him lies, so the man narrates them. It is said: Who told you this? He replies: A man I do not know.'

العربية
Isnad

وَأَخْبَرَنِي شَبِيبٌ، عَنْ رَوْحِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ عَاصِمِ ابْنِ بَهْدَلَةَ، عَنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ "

Matn

إِنَّ الشَّيْطَانَ يَلْقَى الرَّجُلَ فِي صُورَةِ الْإِنْسَانِ فَيُخْبِرُهُ بِالْكَذِبِ فَيُحَدِّثُ بِهِ، فَيُقَالُ: مَنْ حَدَّثَكَ؟ فَيَقُولُ: رَجُلٌ لَا أَعْرِفُهُ "

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Ibn MasudEarliest transmitterعَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ
  2. 02Al-Musayyib b. Rafiعَنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ بْنِ رَافِعٍ
  3. 03Asim b. Bahdalahعَنْ عَاصِمِ ابْنِ بَهْدَلَةَ
  4. 04Rawh b. al-Qasimعَنْ رَوْحِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ

Entry553/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 648
English
Isnad

Yahya b. Ayyub reported to me from al-Muthanna b. al-Sabbah, from ‘Amr b. Shu‘ayb, that Mu‘adh b. Jabal said

Matn

The Messenger of God, ﷺ, was traveling when a man said: 'O Messenger of God, so-and-so does not eat until he is fed, and does not travel until he is prepared for.' The Messenger of God, ﷺ, said: 'You have backbitten your brother.' They said: 'We only said what is true about him.' He replied: 'It is enough that you speak about your brother what is true about him.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ، عَنِ الْمُثَنَّى بْنِ الصَّبَّاحِ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ، أَنَّ

Matn

مُعَاذَ بْنَ جَبَلٍ، قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام مُسَافِرًا، فَقَالَ رَجُلٌ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ مَا يَأْكُلُ فُلَانٌ حَتَّى يُطْعَمَ، وَلَا يَرْحَلُ حَتَّى يُرَحَّلَ لَهُ مَا أَعْجَزَهُ فَقَالَ: «اغْتَبْتُمْ أَخَاكُمْ» ، قَالُوا: قُلْنَا مَا فِيهِ؟ قَالَ: «حَسْبُكُمْ أَنْ تُحَدِّثُوا عَنْ أَخِيكُمْ مَا فِيهِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Muadh b. Jabalأَنَّ مُعَاذَ بْنَ جَبَلٍ
  3. 03Amr b. Shuaibعَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ
  4. 04Al-Muthanna b. al-Sabbahعَنِ الْمُثَنَّى بْنِ الصَّبَاحِ
  5. 05Yahya b. Ayyubيَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي

Entry554/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 649
English
Isnad

Yahya b. Ayyub reported to me from Yahya b. Sa‘id, from ‘Amrah bint ‘Abd al-Rahman,

Matn

that ‘A’ishah bint Talhah b. ‘Ubayd God entered upon ‘A’ishah, the wife of the Prophet, ﷺ, while a Bedouin woman was with her. The Bedouin woman left, dragging her garment. ‘A’ishah bint Talhah said 'How long her garment is!' ‘A’ishah, the wife of the Prophet, ﷺ, said: 'You have backbitten her. Catch up with her and ask her to seek forgiveness for you.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ،

Matn

أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ ابْنَةَ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، دَخَلَتْ عَلَى عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام ، وَعِنْدَهَا أَعْرَابِيَّةٌ، فَخَرَجَتِ الْأَعْرَابِيَّةُ عَلَى ذَيْلِهَا، فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ بِنْتُ طَلْحَةَ: مَا أَطْوَلَ ذَيْلَهَا ، فَقَالَتْ لَهَا عَائِشَةُ، زَوْجُ النَّبِيِّ ، عليه السلام: «اغْتَبْتِيهَا، أَدْرِكِيهَا تَسْتَغْفِرْ لَكِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Aishah, wife of the ProphetEarliest transmitterعَلَى عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ
  2. 02Aishah bint Talhahأَنَّ عَائِشَةَ ابْنَةَ طَلْحَةَ
  3. 03Amrah bint Abd al-Rahmanعَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  4. 04Yahya b. Saidعَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ
  5. 05Yahya b. Ayyubيَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry555/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 651
English
Isnad

Yahya b. Ayyub reported to me from Isma‘il b. ‘Uqbah al-Madani that he heard Ibn Shihab say:

Matn

Ibn Mas‘ud climbed a tree to pick fruit for the Messenger of God, ﷺ. Some of the Prophet’s companions said 'How thin your legs are, O Ibn Umm ‘Abd!' The Messenger of God, ﷺ, said: 'By the One in Whose hand is my soul, they are heavier in the scale than Mount Uhud. And you have backbitten him.'

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ الْمَدَنِيِّ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ، يَقُولُ:

Matn

إِنَّ ابْنَ مَسْعُودٍ طَلَعَ شَجَرَةً يَجْنِيهَا لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، فَقَالَ بَعْضُ أَصْحَابِ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام: مَا أَدَقَّ سَاقَيْكَ يَا ابْنَ أُمِّ عَبْدٍ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام: «وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لَهُمَا فِي الْمِيزَانِ أَثْقَلُ مِنْ أُحُدٍ ، وَقَدِ اغْتَبْتَهُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Ibn ShihabEarliest transmitterأَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ
  2. 02Ismail b. Uqbah al-Madaniعَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ الْمَدَنِيِّ
  3. 03Yahya b. Ayyubيَحْيَى بْنُ أَيُّوبَ
  4. 04Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry556/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 652
English
Isnad

Hisham b. Sa'd and 'Abd al-Rahman b. Zayd b. Aslam reported to me from his father, that the Messenger of God said:

Matn

“A servant will come on the Day of Resurrection with good deeds like mountains. It will be announced: ‘Whoever has a claim against him, let him come and take it.’ Deeds will continue to be taken from his good deeds until nothing remains for him except ‘There is no god but God.’”

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي هِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، وَعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام قَالَ: "

Matn

يَأْتِي الْعَبْدُ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ فَيَكُونُ لَهُ مِنَ الْحَسَنَاتِ أَمْثَالُ الْجِبَالِ، فَيُدْعَى: مَنْ كَانَ لَهُ حَقٌّ فَلْيَأْتِ يَأْخُذْهُ، فَمَا يَزَالُ يُؤْخَذُ مِنْ حَسَنَاتِهِ حَتَّى لَا تَبْقَى لَهُ إِلَّا لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ "

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Zayd b. Aslamعَنْ أَبِيهِ
  3. 03Abd al-Rahman b. Zayd b. Aslam (parallel transmitter)وَعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ
  4. 04Hisham b. Sad (parallel transmitter)هِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي
Source and transmission notes

Hisham b. Sad and Abd al-Rahman b. Zayd b. Aslam are parallel sources from Zayd b. Aslam. Arabic report describes a servant's deeds on the Day of Resurrection; no matching English translation is available after manual realignment. Translation reviewed against Arabic. Translated from Arabic; the source English was not confidently aligned.

Entry557/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 653
English
Isnad

Al-Layth b. Sa'd reported to me from Khalid b. Yazid, from Ibn Abi Hilal, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith, to whom it was conveyed that:

Matn

“While the Prophet was traveling, a Bedouin came to him and began asking him questions, and the Prophet turned toward him. The people said, ‘Doesn’t this Bedouin’s lack of reserve amaze you? He has come between us and the Prophet.’ Then the Bedouin’s camel knocked him down and killed him. The Prophet said, ‘Go down to your companion and bury him. By the One in whose hand is my soul, he has been struck down while your mouths are still dripping with his blood.’”

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي اللَّيْثُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ خَالِدِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي هِلَالٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ عليه السلام

Matn

بَيْنَا هُوَ يَسِيرُ إِذْ أَتَاهُ أَعْرَابِيٌّ فَجَعَلَ يَسْأَلُهُ ، فَأَقْبَلَ عَلَيْهِ النَّبِيُّ عليه السلام، فَقَالَ الْقَوْمُ: أَلَا تُعْجِبُكُمْ قِلَّةُ حَيَاءِ هَذَا الْأَعْرَابِيِّ؟ حَالَ بَيْنَنَا وَبَيْنَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ، فَوَقَصَهُ بَعِيرُهُ فَقَتَلَهُ، فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ عليه السلام: «انْزِلُوا إِلَى صَاحِبِكُمْ فَغَيِّبُوهُ، وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لَقَدْ أُصِيبَ وَإِنَّ أَفْوَاهَكُمْ لَتَنْطُفُ مِنْ دَمِهِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The ProphetEarliest transmitterأَنَّ النَّبِيَّ
  2. 02Unnamed source(s) in a balaghأَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ
  3. 03Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harithعَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ
  4. 04Ibn Abi Hilalعَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي هِلَالٍ
  5. 05Khalid b. Yazidعَنْ خَالِدِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ
  6. 06Al-Layth b. Sadاللَّيْثُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ
  7. 07Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي
Source and transmission notes

Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith reports this as having reached him; the intervening source(s) are omitted. No matching English translation is available after manual realignment. Translation reviewed against Arabic. The report is translated from the surviving Arabic; the source English attached here described another report. Corrected the transmission formula balaghahu as “to whom it was conveyed”; no named intermediary is inferred.

Entry558/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 654
English
Isnad

Jarir b. Hazim reported to me from al-Hasan b. 'Umarah, from Talhah b. 'Ubayd God, from 'A'ishah bint Talhah b. 'Ubayd God, from 'A'ishah, the wife of the Prophet, who said:

Matn

“A short woman came in to see us. When she left, I gestured with my hand [to indicate her height] and said, ‘How short she is!’ The Messenger of God said, ‘You have backbitten her. Get up and ask her to release you from that.’ She said: Another woman came to see us, trailing her garment. When she left, I said, ‘Messenger of God, how long her train is!’ He said, ‘You have backbitten her. Get up and ask her to release you from that.’”

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي جَرِيرُ بْنُ حَازِمٍ، عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنْ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ بِنْتِ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام قَالَتْ:

Matn

دَخَلَتْ عَلَيْنَا امْرَأَةٌ قَصِيرَةٌ، فَلَمَّا خَرَجَتْ قُلْتُ بِيَدِي هَكَذَا: مَا أَقْصَرَهَا، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام: «اغْتَبْتِيهَا، قَوْمِي فَتَحَلَّلِيهَا» ، قَالَتْ: فَدَخَلَتْ عَلَيْنَا امْرَأَةٌ تَجُرُّ ذَيْلَهَا، فَلَمَّا خَرَجَتْ قُلْتُ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مَا أَطْوَلَ ذَيْلَهَا، فَقَالَ: «اغْتَبْتِيهَا ، فَقُومِي فَتَحَلَّلِيهَا»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Aishah, wife of the ProphetEarliest transmitterعَنْ عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ
  2. 02Aishah bint Talhah b. Ubayd Allahعَنْ عَائِشَةَ بِنْتِ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ
  3. 03Talhah b. Ubayd Allahعَنْ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ
  4. 04Al-Hasan b. Umarahعَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ
  5. 05Jarir b. Hazimجَرِيرُ بْنُ حَازِمٍ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَحَدَّثَنِي
Source and transmission notes

Aishah, wife of the Prophet, relates two incidents in the same report about backbiting. No matching English translation is available after manual realignment. Translation reviewed against Arabic. The Arabic is translated directly; the source English was not aligned to this report.

Entry559/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 655
English
Isnad

Hafs b. Maysarah and Hisham b. Sa'd reported to me from Zayd b. Aslam

Matn

that, during the illness from which the Messenger of God died, his wives gathered around him. Safiyyah bint Huyayy said, ‘By God, O Prophet of God, I wish that what afflicts you were afflicting me.’ The other wives exchanged glances at her. The Messenger of God noticed them and said, ‘Rinse your mouths.’ They asked, ‘From what, O Messenger of God?’ He replied, ‘From your exchanging glances at your companion. By God, she is truthful.’

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي حَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ، وَهِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام فِي وَجَعِهِ الَّذِي تُوُفِّيَ مِنْهُ

Matn

اجْتَمَعَ إِلَيْهِ نِسَاؤُهُ، فَقَالَتْ صَفِيَّةُ بِنْتُ حُيَيٍّ: أَمَا وَاللَّهِ يَا نَبِيَّ اللَّهِ لَوَدِدْتُ أَنَّ الَّذِي بِكَ بِي، فَتَغَامَزْنَهَا أَزْوَاجُ النَّبِيِّ عليه السلام ، فَأَبْصَرَهُنَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام فَقَالَ: «مَضْمِضْنَ» ، قُلْنَ: مِنْ أَيِّ شَيْءٍ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ؟ قَالَ: «مِنْ تَغَامُزِكُنَّ بِصَاحِبَتِكُنَّ، وَاللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ إِنَّهَا صَادِقَةٌ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Zayd b. Aslamعَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ
  3. 03Hisham b. Sad (parallel transmitter)وَهِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ
  4. 04Hafs b. Maysarah (parallel transmitter)حَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ
  5. 05Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry560/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 656
English
Isnad

Hafs b. Maysarah and Hisham b. Sa‘d reported to me from Zayd b. Aslam that

Matn

two men passed by the Messenger of God, ﷺ, while he was sitting in the Hijr, and another man was standing and praying. They exchanged glances at him. The Messenger of God, ﷺ, heard their words while they were circumambulating. When they returned to him, he handed them a stick and said 'Use it to clean your teeth.' They said: 'O Prophet of God, we did not eat anything.' He replied: 'Yes, you did.'

العربية
Isnad

وَأَخْبَرَنِي حَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ، وَهِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، "

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلَيْنِ، مَرَّا بِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ عليه السلام وَهُوَ قَاعِدٌ فِي الْحِجْرِ، وَرَجُلٌ قَائِمٌ يُصَلِّي يَتَغَامَزَانِهِ، فَسَمِعَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عليه السلام قَوْلَهُمَا وَهُمَا فِي الطَّوَافِ، فَلَمَّا رَجَعَا إِلَيْهِ نَاوَلَهُمَا عُودًا فَقَالَ «تَخَلَّلَا بِهِ» ، فَقَالَا: يَا نَبِيَّ اللَّهِ، مَا أَكَلْنَاهُ، فَقَالَ: «بَلْ قَدْ أَكَلْتُمَاهُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Zayd b. AslamEarliest transmitterعَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ
  2. 02Hisham b. Sad (parallel transmitter)وَهِشَامُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ
  3. 03Hafs b. Maysarah (parallel transmitter)حَفْصُ بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ
  4. 04Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)وَأَخْبَرَنِي

Entry561/ 148
Al-Jāmiʿ fī al-Ḥadīth of Ibn Wahb · Vol. 1, p. 657
English
Isnad

Hafs b. Maysarah reported to me from al-'Ala' b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from his father, from Abu Hurayrah, that the Messenger of God, ﷺ, said:

Matn

‘Do you know who the bankrupt is?’ They replied, ‘The bankrupt among us is one who has no dirham.’ He said, ‘The bankrupt of my community is one who comes on the Day of Resurrection with prayer, fasting, and alms, but has insulted one person, slandered another, struck another, and consumed another’s wealth. He will then sit, and this one will be given some of his good deeds, and that one some of his good deeds. If his good deeds run out before he has settled the wrongs he owes, some of their sins will be taken and cast upon him; then he will be cast into the Fire.’

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي حَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ، عَنِ الْعَلَاءِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ عليه السلام ، قَالَ: «

Matn

أَتَدْرُونَ مَا الْمُفْلِسُ؟» قَالُوا: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ الْمُفْلِسُ فِينَا مَنْ لَا يَمْلِكُ دِرْهَمًا لَهُ، قَالَ: «الْمُفْلِسُ مِنْ أُمَّتِي يَأْتِي يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ بِصِيَامٍ وَصَلَاةٍ وَزَكَاةٍ ، وَيَأْتِي قَدْ قَذَفَ هَذَا، وَشَتَمَ عِرْضَ هَذَا وَضَرَبَ هَذَا وَأَكَلَ مَالَ هَذَا، فَيَقْعُدُ فَيُعْطَى هَذَا مِنْ حَسَنَاتِهِ، وَهَذَا مِنْ حَسَنَاتِهِ، فَإِنْ فَنِيَتْ حَسَنَاتُهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْضِيَ الَّذِي عَلَيْهِ مِنَ الْخَطَايَا أُخِذَ مِنْ خَطَايَاهُمْ فَطُرِحْنَ عَلَيْهِ، ثُمَّ طُرِحَ فِي النَّارِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01The Messenger of AllahEarliest transmitterأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ
  2. 02Abu Hurayrahعَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ
  3. 03His father, Abd al-Rahman b. Yaqubعَنْ أَبِيهِ
  4. 04Al-Ala b. Abd al-Rahmanعَنِ الْعَلَاءِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  5. 05Hafs b. Maysarahحَفْصُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ
  6. 06Ibn Wahb (compiler; contextual first-person narrator)قَالَ وَأَخْبَرَنِي
